# MIST / LINEAGEOS 23.2 — LIVE PROJECT STATUS AND NEXT-CHAT HANDOVER

**Last reviewed: 14 November 2026 (Malaysia time).**  
**Start every new chat here:** `copilot_project/PROJECT_STATUS.md`. **Workflow execution rule: trigger, provide run link, and do not monitor unless user requests investigation.** Check GitHub Actions for later runs and update this file with **verified** changes before ending a development session. This is a living Markdown status file. For an explicit **HANDOVER**, also generate a proper, updated PDF; do not merely reuse an older PDF.

## 1. Goal and strict rules

Bring up Android 16 **LineageOS 23.2** for **Redmi Note 17 5G / mist**, platform `parrot` / Qualcomm SM4450, based on stock Xiaomi Global **OS3.0.304.0.WPUMIXM**. Firmware MD5 `c4450c4e168052ca9de1211306d3beff`.

- **ONLY existing `main` branches. NO pull requests, NO new branches.**
- All GitHub Actions workflows, experiments, research scripts, temporary tools, and downloaded or staged archives must live **only** in `nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project`.
- Production device, vendor and kernel trees receive **only device-specific, evidence-reviewed source and necessary files**; never put Actions or experimental scripts in them.
- The `sm4450-development` GitHub organization is **architectural reference only**. Never blindly copy another device's partitions, DTBO, SELinux, proprietary blobs or device settings. MiCode `mist-w-oss` is now separately confirmed as **device-specific upstream OSS kernel source**, not a validated LineageOS vendor configuration.
- `GH_PAT` exists as a secret in `copilot_project`. Never disclose it, paste it in code or logs, or assume publish permissions before verification. The research-only workflows use the GitHub Actions token.
- Never publish `persist`, `modemst`, individual phone calibration, private identifiers, signing keys or other sensitive partitions. No stock bloat wholesale.
- Do not wipe or replace the user's existing local LineageOS checkout until read-only inventory and backup.
- A successful *research job* is **not** a successful Lineage build or device boot.

### Important source data

- Stock firmware Android 16, build `OS3.0.304.0.WPUMIXM`; `mist`, `mist_global`, SM4450.
- Stock kernel `6.6.118-android15-8-g666ad52b8230-ab15657065-4k`; physical `super` size from fastboot is 10,200,547,328 bytes, **not** necessarily the dynamic group usable size.
- Eight populated dynamic images: `system_a`, `system_ext_a`, `product_a`, `vendor_a`, `vendor_dlkm_a`, `system_dlkm_a`, `odm_a`, `mi_ext_a`.
- Stock filesystem `/data`: F2FS with wrapped-key metadata encryption; stock `/vendor`: EROFS. The fstab carries both EROFS and EXT4 alternatives, AVB chaining and HyperOS overlays. **Do not copy overlays blindly.**
- Verified stock boot chain: `boot.img`, `init_boot.img`, `vendor_boot.img`, `recovery.img`, `dtbo.img`, `vbmeta.img`, `vbmeta_system.img`.
- Stock artifact archives and review artifacts from 12 November expire **26 November 2026** unless separately preserved or regenerated.

### Membership evidence (not blob approval)

From the published `mist-archive-membership.json`:
- 4,373 tar archive members; 4,352 allowed names; 21 rejected paths by safety filter.
- 198 init executable declarations under vendor/ODM; **180 present, 18 absent references**, with some duplicate executable paths.
- 105 stock VINTF documents with HAL declarations: **104 file paths present, 1 absent**. The absent path is `vendor_a/etc/vintf/manifest/android.hardware.security.keymint-service-qti.xml`; it declares KeyMint, SecureClock, SharedSecret in the original parsed config. This does **not** establish that these HALs are absent from runtime.
- The 18 missing service references include `/vendor/bin/hw/rild`, `/vendor/bin/ATFWD-daemon`, `/vendor/bin/wifi_qos_daemon`, plus legacy/factory/optional candidates. Do **not** add broken binaries or delete init services based only on the name inventory.
- `modules.load` checks: system_dlkm flattened 78, system_dlkm versioned 78, vendor_dlkm 350; **zero missing .ko basenames** in archive inventory. Note 350 vendor load entries but 268 distinct `modules.dep` records. Basename-only matches prove neither module ABI nor load compatibility.

### Newly resolved research detail: 12 November 2026

- Run [43185479167](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project/actions/runs/43185479167), job `131071453344`: **SUCCESS**. The script `scripts/reconcile_missing_stock_paths.py` and `.github/workflows/missing-stock-path-review.yml` are in `copilot_project` only; workflow saved two new reports to `research/generated/`.
- **18 missing init service declarations correspond to 16 distinct executable paths**, so avoid double-counting `rild` and `LKCore` from multiple RC declarations.
- Missing `vendor_a/bin/hw/rild`: a different binary `vendor_a/bin/hw/qcrilNrd` exists; **not validated as a drop-in replacement**. Need inspect active RIL/IMS services, RC files, ELF dependencies and runtime SKU.
- Missing `vendor_a/bin/init.qti.fm.sh`: same basename exists at `vendor_a/etc/init.qti.fm.sh`; **path mismatch**, not proof that this stock RC service launches as written.
- Missing `vendor_a/bin/boringssl_self_test32`: `vendor_a/bin/boringssl_self_test64` exists. Architecture mismatch means **do not substitute automatically**.
- Missing `vendor_a/bin/check_keybox`: `vendor_a/bin/StoreKeybox` and `vendor_a/bin/spu_install_keybox` are filename-near alternatives only, not verified equivalents.
- KeyMint VINTF XML exact path remains absent from the reviewed stock vendor archive. **13 related KeyMint/Keymaster filenames are libraries**, such as `libqtikeymint.so` and `libspukeymint.so`; no service executable or registration can be inferred from these names. Review stock/active VINTF fragment loading, binary location, SELinux and KeyStore.
- Module basenames and original 350 vendor `modules.load` entries remain **unverified for ABI, dependency resolution and duplicate semantics**.
- **NEXT engineering gate**: read-only stock file metadata + symlink-target/hash inventory and recursive ELF `DT_NEEDED` closure for a tightly chosen set (RIL/IMS, KeyMint, fingerprint, audio, camera); then curated proprietary manifest. No blanket vendor copying.

### Verified HAL dependency report — engineering interpretation

- `research/generated/mist-five-hal-dependencies.json` is available on `main` after successful run 43192475984. It includes direct and recursive dependency edges plus exact stock archive paths, but still lacks full linker-namespace and ABI validation.

- The previous first batch of 27 production files is unchanged by this research workflow; ROM compilation and runtime validation are still pending.

It checks `boot.img` SHA256 against the stock artifact hash file, parses v3/v4 boot header, validates bounds and kernel format, extracts **only unchanged kernel payload** and writes SHA256/header metadata. Both cross-repository git commits explicitly use **`nobodyAtall-code <hashyofficial@gmail.com>`**. Only the `copilot_project` repo contains workflow/research scripts.

### Validated kernel payload metadata — stock Android 16

- `boot.img` verified source SHA256: `45ba1fec1fbacb06c182f546d63b5a4b2bf54627094745bff5e208eb72b1b579`.
- Android boot header **v4**, size **1,584 bytes**; kernel begins at **offset 4,096**. Kernel type: **uncompressed ARM64 Image**, exact size **36,801,024 bytes**, SHA256: `b7826ac3d30f03a73ce3d6994ef92914c214781f408741cda72939511eff51dc`. Stock `boot.img` ramdisk size **0** (separate `init_boot` is known to exist).
- **The prebuilt kernel repository is now populated** and preliminary `BoardConfig.mk` points to its kernel payload. This does **not** validate kernel modules, dtbo, vendor_boot ramdisk, AVB, recovery or complete ROM building.
- Next focus: inspect `vendor_boot.img` and `dtbo.img` against stock device module inventory, determine actual super dynamic group capacities, and validate required device boot/partition config before attempting first `lunch lineage_mist-userdebug`.

### 12 November 2026 — kernel config runs; missing Xiaomi proprietary MIHW source

- [Run 43203333761](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project/actions/runs/43203333761), job [131132112190](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project/actions/runs/43203333761/job/131132112190), **FAILED**. Pinned Google manifest dependency bootstrap and nine Xiaomi-source overlays passed; Bazel query passed and `--define=FACTORY_BUILD=0` cleared previous configuration analysis error. Bazel build reached **468/473 actions** and entered kernel `mist_perf` Kconfig generation. No kernel binary was built.
- Exact failure: `drivers/Kconfig:248: can't open file "drivers/mihw/Kconfig"`. The pinned owned kernel `drivers/mihw` is **a symlink** to `../../../vendor/xiaomi/proprietary/mihw` (identical in official `MiCode/Xiaomi_Kernel_OpenSource` `mist-w-oss`), while current pinned build workspace does **not** contain `vendor/xiaomi/proprietary/mihw/Kconfig`. This is a **missing proprietary Xiaomi source tree**, not the previously fixed `FACTORY_BUILD` issue.
- Source discovery did not establish a trusted, Mist-compatible official MIHW source repository or pinned revision. Avoid silently replacing MIHW with unrelated older device trees or stubbing/deleting Kconfig; any such action could compromise correctness and stock module compatibility. Original kernel and stock prebuilt remain unchanged. As of this update **no new corrective workflow run** has been started for this missing-source failure.
- Next milestone: obtain the correct Mist `vendor/xiaomi/proprietary/mihw` source tree (and pin its exact commit and provenance) or establish an explicitly marked diagnostic-only workaround if full sources are unavailable. Overlay at the correct symlink destination, verify required Kconfig and drivers, then re-run Kleaf build. Do not assert full kernel, ROM or boot success.
- Build efficiency: the previous sync of fresh Android 15/Linux 6.6 dependencies took several minutes per runner. Repeated runs before MIHW source identification are wasteful; consider a preflight source-completeness check and a verified cache strategy.

### 12 November 2026 — diagnostic-only MIHW Kconfig guard to continue compiler discovery

- The real kernel configuration failure in run 43203333761 was missing `drivers/mihw/Kconfig`. The official Xiaomi kernel `mist-w-oss` publishes `drivers/mihw` as a relative symlink to `vendor/xiaomi/proprietary/mihw`, which is **not** in the nine pinned public-source checkouts. The missing Kconfig is therefore a structurally unresolved external dependency; no verified official Mist MIHW source was found. Do not conflate user-supplied web summaries with direct source verification.
- Inspected the published kernel `drivers/Kconfig`, `drivers/Makefile`, `arch/arm64/configs/gki_defconfig`, and `arch/arm64/configs/vendor/mist_perf.config`. The published `drivers/Makefile` gates MIHW object compilation with `obj-$(CONFIG_MIHW) += mihw/`; neither the base GKI defconfig nor Mist perf fragment explicitly enables `CONFIG_MIHW`.
- **Experimental diagnostic workaround only:** [commit 3ad4167](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project/commit/3ad4167f6b0fd699e05e664159ccb1812a025e33) adds a build-time guard **inside the disposable GitHub Actions runner**: asserts both config fragments have no `CONFIG_MIHW=` assignment; asserts the symlink and missing public source; replaces the one `source "drivers/mihw/Kconfig"` line with a clearly labeled diagnostic comment. This does not fabricate proprietary source and does not modify the owned kernel, vendor tree, stock prebuilt, or official MiCode source. Other missing Kconfigs and source dependencies may remain.
- **Safety:** Any resulting `Image`, DTB, DTBO, module or build success is **experimental and NOT stock/boot/ABI validated**. The workflow JSON deliberately reports `diagnostic_missing_mihw_guard=true`, `kernel_compiled=false`, `validated_stock_abi=false`, and `boot_tested=false`, with `experimental_kernel_compiled` for diagnostic completion only. Never deploy these artifacts to a device without independent source/module compatibility review.
- New [run 43207575528](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project/actions/runs/43207575528) **in progress at last check**. Inspect logs for additional fatal dependencies and move toward original-source-preserving build and stock ABI verification. Treat restoring missing official MIHW source as the preferred production path.
- Known limitation: this run still performs full pinned Android kernel dependency sync on each fresh hosted runner (several minutes), and its outcome or speed improvement has not been verified.

### 12 November 2026 — first actual diagnostic C compilation; direct MIHW scheduler dependency

Many DT compiler warnings (including mist overlay reg_format) also appeared; these are separate from the C fatal error and should be assessed rather than silently assumed safe.
- [Commit c6e4cc7](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project/commit/c6e4cc7c29db287bfef75adcb639152f815e961b) updates **only** `copilot_project` diagnostic workflow: asserts expected original `CONFIG_METIS_WALT=y` and omits it from the disposable runner-only defconfig fragment, alongside six previously omitted MIHW-related switches. All production source/config and stock prebuilt remain unchanged.
- **Critical distinction:** this is NOT stock-compatible. Real MIHW source is required for a valid production compilation and eventual ABI/module validation. Do not flash or publish any output from these MIHW-disabled experiments. Verify the next run's exact error and do not declare success based on compilation progress alone.

### Workflow handoff rule — 13 November 2026 (user instruction)

**For future chats and all subsequent development milestones:** after implementing and committing a new GitHub Actions workflow or a workflow fix directly to existing `copilot_project/main`, **launch/trigger the workflow and share its exact run link, then stop**. **Do not monitor, poll, wait for completion, or repeatedly inspect its logs/results** by default. The user will send the run link if it fails or when they want the result investigated. Only inspect a completed run when explicitly requested, and then implement the next fix/milestone accordingly. This supersedes older next-chat instructions that automatically ask for status monitoring. Always update `PROJECT_STATUS.md` with what was pushed and the launched run ID, marking execution results **pending/unverified** until the user supplies outcome for review. ### 14 November 2026 — repeated targeted test envsetup nounset: inherited SHELLOPTS fixed

- User reran targeted `test_mist_selected_hal_modules.sh` after first `set -euo pipefail` -> `set -eo pipefail` change, but still got `build/envsetup.sh: line 21: TOP: unbound variable`; targeted HAL compilation had not started.

Its \`Android.bp\` has \`qti_generate_display_kernel_headers\` genrule producing the exact four missing include names \`display/drm/sde_drm.h\`, \`display/drm/msm_drm_pp.h\`, \`display/media/mmm_color_fmt.h\`, \`display/media/msm_sde_rotator.h\` (plus \`mi_disp.h\` and HDCP header); verified the first four exist under \`include/uapi/display/{drm,media}\`. \`display_kernel_headers.py\` is also present.

### 2026-11-14 — Corrected mi_ext recommendation using ACTUAL Mist files + AOSP update_engine contract

- A clean LineageOS `super`/OTA design **without a generated `mi_ext.img` is plausible and preferred for validation**, after auditing the existing stock content; earlier preference for retaining `mi_ext` by analogy to unbooted Xiaomi Madrid was overgeneralized.
- Reopened the actual **22-report ZIP** from complete successful firmware workflow [43579076486](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project/actions/runs/43579076486/job/132399705358), parsed `all-files.tsv`: stock `mi_ext_a` contains **150 indexed file paths**, chiefly 61 `product/etc`, 22 `product/opcust`, 19 `product/priv-app`, 11 `product/data-app`, 10 `product/app`, nine `product/ai`, six `product/overlay`, four `etc/init` plus eight other files. No listed `.ko`, `.so` or `.mbn` paths; the six overlays include `GmsTelephonyOverlay.apk`, `GmsTelecommOverlay.apk`, `MiuiTelephonyResCustOverlay.apk`, `MiuiStkResOverlay.apk`. Path inventory alone cannot prove RRO targeting or the safety of dropping regional/telephony config.
- **Authoritative AOSP semantics**: `update_engine` reconstructs target-slot dynamic groups from OTA manifest, keeping source-slot extents; partitions may be deleted *when managed in the target group*, but **groups absent from the payload manifest remain untouched** per `update_metadata.proto`. Thus the Google-generated claim that merely omitting `mi_ext` in BoardConfig automatically deletes it from the active slot is unproven. Also `BOARD_GOOGLE_DYNAMIC_PARTITIONS_PARTITION_LIST` is not the variable shown by current Qualcomm Lineage trees; `BOARD_QTI_DYNAMIC_PARTITIONS_PARTITION_LIST` is relevant when group is `qti_dynamic_partitions`.
- Stock `vendor_boot` carries original first-stage `fstab.default` with `mi_ext` `nofail`, bind and numerous overlay mounts. Removing `mi_ext` from the **uninstalled** staged `rootdir/etc/fstab.mist` does NOT remove that stock ramdisk's references. Never blindly delete original stock boot structures or ignore boot warnings.
- Added concrete device-tree report [MIST_MI_EXT_ACTUAL_CONTENT_AND_AOSP_OTA_2026-11-14.md](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/android_device_xiaomi_mist/blob/main/research/MIST_MI_EXT_ACTUAL_CONTENT_AND_AOSP_OTA_2026-11-14.md) (commit `62912a1`) and corrected earlier comparison report (commit `d603abf`). No production layout, kernel, or ramdisk changes have yet been made from this analysis, no new workflow/run required; only after verifying candidate contents and actual OTA metadata should the ROM drop `mi_ext`.

### 2026-11-15 — VINTF wrapper OUT variable collision after successful lunch

- User supplied third `mist-vintf-build-report(3).txt` and terminal: launcher `mist-vintf-nounset-v3-20261115` starts with shell flags `ehBE` (nounset OFF), successfully sources `build/envsetup.sh` and lunches `lineage_mist-bp4a-userdebug` for Android 16/LineageOS 23.2. It prints `===== BUILD TARGET: check-vintf-all =====` then exits prematurely with `tee: /home/hashysingh/android/lineage/out/target/product/mist: Is a directory`. No actual check-vintf-all compilation result has been reported.
- **Identified real root cause**: script declared `OUT="$ROOT/mist-vintf-build-report.txt"` before `lunch`. Android's sourced build functions reuse/overwrite shell variable `OUT` to denote the device product output directory. The script's later `tee -a "$OUT"` therefore attempted to append to a directory, triggering exit under `set -e`. This is a launcher fault, NOT a ROM/VINTF validation error.
- Fixed every wrapper use of `OUT`, `JSON`, `TRACE` to isolated names `MIST_VINTF_REPORT_PATH`, `MIST_VINTF_RESULT_PATH`, `MIST_VINTF_BUILD_TRACE_PATH` and version-stamped `mist-vintf-path-isolation-v4-20261115`. Published [commit 1037af9](https://github.com/nobodyAtall-code/copilot_project/commit/1037af967b3043cc40a56a3c066df1f70284f482). Original source trees untouched.
